// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The main cost is candidate-cluster construction, and each seed is independent within a QT iteration. I’m going to combine coarse-grained OpenMP seed parallelism with an incremental diameter update: each candidate’s distance to the growing cluster is updated only for the newly added member, eliminating repeated recomputation without changing the greedy selection order. Tie-breaking will remain explicitly by the original seed order so output does not depend on thread scheduling.

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
-    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
+    std::vector<std::uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
-    
-    // Initialize unclustered indices
+
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
+        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
     }
-    
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
+            {
+                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+                    for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    finished = false;
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
-    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
+    std::vector<std::uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;

-    // Initialize unclustered indices

-        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
+        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
     }
-    
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N,

-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
+            {
+                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+                    for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    finished = false;
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

The first correctness checks match all recorded sequential hashes exactly at 100, 300, 500, and 1,000 points, including validation. The 1,000-point run dropped from about 7.0 seconds to about 0.09 seconds with eight threads on this host; I’m now checking determinism across thread counts and measuring scaling on a larger workload to catch synchronization or load-balancing issues.

-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
+            {
+                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+                    for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    finished = false;
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;

-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
+            {
+                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+                    for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    finished = false;
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;

     }
-    
+
     return clusters;

+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);

+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;

+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;

+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)

+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }

+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+                    for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());

+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }

-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),

-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;

+

codex
A stress run exposed an intermittent synchronization hang at higher thread counts in the persistent-team version. The computation and hashes are correct, but that behavior is unacceptable; I’m simplifying the round synchronization to remove the risky interaction while retaining parallel seed evaluation and deterministic reduction, then I’ll rerun the thread-count sweep.
apply patch

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);

+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;

+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});

+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());

+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }

-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
-    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
+    std::vector<std::uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
-    
-    // Initialize unclustered indices
+
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
+        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
     }
-    
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
+            {
+                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+                    for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    finished = false;
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {

+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }

+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {

+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;

+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());

+                best_seed = -1;

+            int local_best_seed = -1;

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
-    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
+    std::vector<std::uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
-    
-    // Initialize unclustered indices
+
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
+        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
     }
-    
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed

+
+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
+            {
+                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+                    for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    finished = false;
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

   253	// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
   254	bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
   255	                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
   256	                     const double threshold) {
   257	    bool valid = true;
   258	    
   259	    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
   260	    
   261	    // Check each cluster
   262	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   263	        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
   264	        double max_diameter = 0.0;
   265	        
   266	        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
   267	        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
   268	            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
   269	                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
   270	                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
   271	                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
   272	            }
   273	        }
   274	        
   275	        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
   276	            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
   277	                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
   278	        }
   279	        
   280	        // Validate diameter is within threshold
   281	        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
   282	            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
   283	                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
   284	            valid = false;
   285	        }
   286	    }
   287	    
   288	    // Check for duplicate memberships
   289	    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
   290	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
-    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
+    std::vector<std::uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
-    
-    // Initialize unclustered indices
+
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
+        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
     }
-    
-    // Main clustering loop
-    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
-        int max_cardinality = -1;
-        int best_seed = -1;
-        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
+    }
+
+    int max_cardinality = -1;
+    int best_seed = -1;
+    int round_size = 0;
+    bool finished = false;
+    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+
+    // Keep one team alive for all clustering rounds. Candidate seeds within a
+    // round are independent; only the deterministic best candidate is merged.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, clusters, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        finished, best_cluster_members)
+    {
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace(N);
+        std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+        while (true) {
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+                round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+                max_cardinality = -1;
+                best_seed = -1;
+                best_cluster_members.clear();
+            }
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
+            }
+
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            local_best_members.clear();
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
+            {
+                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }
+
+#pragma omp barrier
+#pragma omp single
+            {
+                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+                    clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+                    for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                                       unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                    finished = false;
+                } else {
+                    finished = true;
+                }
             }
-        }
-        
-        // If we found a cluster, add it
-        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
-            Cluster cluster;
-            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
-            clusters.push_back(cluster);
-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered
-            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
+
+            if (finished) {
+                break;
             }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

 // Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point

 // Main QT clustering algorithm
 std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                   const double threshold) {
     const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
-    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
-    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
+    std::vector<std::uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);
     std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
-    
-    // Initialize unclustered indices
+
     for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
+        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
+    }
+
+    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
+    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
+    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
+        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
+            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
+        }
     }
-    
-    // Main clustering loop
+
+    // Allocate one reusable scratch area per possible worker. This avoids all
+    // allocation in the hot seed loop while allowing a fresh parallel region
+    // at each algorithmic round (the rounds themselves are data-dependent).
+    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces;
+    workspaces.reserve(omp_get_max_threads());
+    for (int i = 0; i < omp_get_max_threads(); ++i) {
+        workspaces.emplace_back(N);
+    }
+
     while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
         int max_cardinality = -1;
         int best_seed = -1;
+        const int round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
         std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
-        
-        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
-            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
-            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
-            
-            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
-            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
-                                                       threshold, N, 
-                                                       &candidate_members);
-            
-            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
-                max_cardinality = cardinality;
-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+
+        // Candidate seeds within a round are independent. Dynamic assignment
+        // balances seeds that grow clusters of substantially different sizes.
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
+        unclustered_indices, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
+        best_cluster_members, workspaces)
+        {
+            CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()];
+            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
+            int local_best_seed = -1;
+            std::vector<int> local_best_members;
+
+#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
+                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best_seed = seed;
+                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
+                }
             }
+
+#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
+            {
+                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
+                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
+                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
+                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
+                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
+                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
+                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
+                }
+            }

-

-            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;

-            
-            // Mark all members as clustered

-                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
-            }
-            
-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
-            unclustered_indices.erase(
-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()
-            );
-        } else {
-            // No more clusters can be formed
+
+        if (best_seed < 0 || max_cardinality <= 0) {
             break;
         }
+
+        clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
+        for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+            clustered[member] = 1;
+        }
+        unclustered_indices.erase(
+            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
+                           unclustered_indices.end(),
+                           [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                               return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                           }),
+            unclustered_indices.end());
     }
-    
+
     return clusters;
 }

                                   const double threshold) {

+    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces;

codex
The revised synchronization is stable from 1 through 32 threads and also completes correctly with the host’s default 256-thread setting. On 2,000 points it scales from 4.88 seconds at one thread to 0.76 seconds at eight threads (about 6.4×), with the same hash at every thread count; the final clean build and validation pass as well.

    12	#include <cstring>
    13	#include <cstdint>
    14	#include <limits>
    15	#include <vector>
    16	
    17	#include <omp.h>
