    19	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    20	
    21	static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
    22	static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
    23	
    24	// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
    25	struct Point {
    26	    double x, y;
    27	};
    28	
    29	// Structure to represent a cluster
    30	struct Cluster {
    31	    std::vector<int> members;
    32	    int seed_point;
    33	};
    34	
    35	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
    36	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    37	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    38	    
    39	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    40	    int count = 0;
    41	    
    42	    while (count < N) {
    43	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
    44	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
    45	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
    46	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
    47	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
    48	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
    49	        
    50	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
    51	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
    52	            group_cnt = N - count;
    53	        }
    54	        
    55	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
    56	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
    57	            const double r = frand() * R;
    58	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
    59	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
    60	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
    61	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
    62	            
    63	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
    64	                continue;
    65	            }
    66	            
    67	            points[count] = {x, y};
    68	            count++;
    69	            group_cnt--;
    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	}
    73	
    74	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    75	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    76	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    77	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    78	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    79	}
    80	
    81	struct CandidateWorkspace {
    82	    explicit CandidateWorkspace(const int point_count)
    83	        : in_cluster(point_count, 0), max_distance(point_count, 0.0) {
    84	        members.reserve(point_count);
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    std::vector<std::uint8_t> in_cluster;
    88	    std::vector<double> max_distance;
    89	    std::vector<int> members;
    90	};
    91	
    92	// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
    93	// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
    94	int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
    95	                              const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& clustered,
    96	                              const std::vector<double>& distances,
    97	                              const double threshold,
    98	                              const int point_count,
    99	                              CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {
   100	    std::fill(workspace.in_cluster.begin(), workspace.in_cluster.end(), 0);
   101	    std::fill(workspace.max_distance.begin(), workspace.max_distance.end(), 0.0);
   102	    workspace.members.clear();
   103	
   104	    // Add seed point
   105	    workspace.in_cluster[seed_point] = 1;
   106	    workspace.members.push_back(seed_point);
   107	
   108	    // The original implementation recalculated a candidate's distance to every
   109	    // member after each insertion.  Only the distance to the newest member can
   110	    // change that maximum, so retain it and update it incrementally.  Scanning
   111	    // candidates in index order and using strict comparisons preserves the
   112	    // original greedy choice and tie breaking exactly.
   113	    int newest_member = seed_point;
   114	    while (static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size()) < point_count) {
   115	        int closest = -1;
   116	        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
   117	        const double* const newest_distances =
   118	            distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(newest_member) * point_count;
   119	
   120	        for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
   121	            if (clustered[candidate] || workspace.in_cluster[candidate]) {
   122	                continue;
   123	            }
   124	
   125	            const double candidate_diameter = std::max(
   126	                workspace.max_distance[candidate], newest_distances[candidate]);
   127	            workspace.max_distance[candidate] = candidate_diameter;
   128	            if (candidate_diameter < threshold && candidate_diameter < min_diameter) {
   129	                min_diameter = candidate_diameter;
   130	                closest = candidate;
   131	            }
   132	        }
   133	
   134	        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
   135	
   136	        workspace.in_cluster[closest] = 1;
   137	        workspace.members.push_back(closest);
   138	        newest_member = closest;
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    return static_cast<int>(workspace.members.size());
   142	}
   143	
   144	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   145	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   146	                                  const double threshold) {
   147	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   148	    std::vector<std::uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);
   149	    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);
   150	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   151	
   152	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   153	        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
   154	    }
   155	
   156	    // Distances are invariant and are heavily reused by every candidate seed.
   157	    // A dense row-major table makes the incremental update a contiguous scan.
   158	    std::vector<double> distances(static_cast<std::size_t>(N) * N);
   159	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   160	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   161	        double* const row = distances.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(i) * N;
   162	        for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
   163	            row[j] = distance(points[i], points[j]);
   164	        }
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    // Allocate one reusable scratch area per possible worker. This avoids all
   168	    // allocation in the hot seed loop while allowing a fresh parallel region
   169	    // at each algorithmic round (the rounds themselves are data-dependent).
   170	    std::vector<CandidateWorkspace> workspaces;
   171	    workspaces.reserve(omp_get_max_threads());
   172	    for (int i = 0; i < omp_get_max_threads(); ++i) {
   173	        workspaces.emplace_back(N);
   174	    }
   175	
   176	    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
   177	        int max_cardinality = -1;
   178	        int best_seed = -1;
   179	        const int round_size = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
   180	        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
   181	
   182	        // Candidate seeds within a round are independent. Dynamic assignment
   183	        // balances seeds that grow clusters of substantially different sizes.
   184	#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(N, threshold, clustered, distances, \
   185	        unclustered_indices, max_cardinality, best_seed, round_size, \
   186	        best_cluster_members, workspaces)
   187	        {
   188	            CandidateWorkspace& workspace = workspaces[omp_get_thread_num()];
   189	            int local_max_cardinality = -1;
   190	            int local_best_seed = -1;
   191	            std::vector<int> local_best_members;
   192	
   193	#pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
   194	            for (int i = 0; i < round_size; ++i) {
   195	                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
   196	                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
   197	                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, N, workspace);
   198	
   199	                if (cardinality > local_max_cardinality ||
   200	                    (cardinality == local_max_cardinality && seed < local_best_seed)) {
   201	                    local_max_cardinality = cardinality;
   202	                    local_best_seed = seed;
   203	                    local_best_members = workspace.members;
   204	                }
   205	            }
   206	
   207	#pragma omp critical(qt_best_candidate)
   208	            {
   209	                if (local_max_cardinality > max_cardinality ||
   210	                    (local_max_cardinality == max_cardinality &&
   211	                     local_best_seed >= 0 &&
   212	                     (best_seed < 0 || local_best_seed < best_seed))) {
   213	                    max_cardinality = local_max_cardinality;
   214	                    best_seed = local_best_seed;
   215	                    best_cluster_members = local_best_members;
   216	                }
   217	            }
   218	        }
   219	
   220	        if (best_seed < 0 || max_cardinality <= 0) {
   221	            break;
   222	        }
   223	
   224	        clusters.push_back({best_cluster_members, best_seed});
   225	        for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
   226	            clustered[member] = 1;
   227	        }
   228	        unclustered_indices.erase(
   229	            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(),
   230	                           unclustered_indices.end(),
   231	                           [&clustered](const int idx) {
   232	                               return clustered[idx] != 0;
   233	                           }),
   234	            unclustered_indices.end());
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    return clusters;
   238	}

   250	        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
   251	        double max_diameter = 0.0;
   252	        
   253	        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
   254	        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
   255	            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {

codex
Implemented OpenMP parallelization while preserving deterministic QT clustering semantics.

Key changes:

- Parallelized distance-table construction and candidate-seed evaluation.
- Added dynamic scheduling for load balancing.
- Reused per-thread workspaces to avoid hot-loop allocations.
- Incrementally updated cluster diameters instead of recomputing them.
- Preserved original seed-order tie-breaking across all thread counts.
- Made OpenMP a required CMake dependency; executable remains `qtclustering`.

Modified:

- [qtclustering.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_omp_r4/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp:81)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_omp_r4/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt:8)

Verification:

- Clean release build succeeds without warnings.
- Validation passes.
- Hashes match the sequential baseline for all tested inputs.
- Deterministic from 1 through 32 threads.
- 2,000-point benchmark scaled from 4.88 seconds with one thread to 0.76 seconds with eight threads, approximately 6.4× faster.

+        for (const int member : best_cluster_members) {
+            clustered[member] = 1;

Implemented OpenMP parallelization while preserving deterministic QT clustering semantics.

Key changes:

- Parallelized distance-table construction and candidate-seed evaluation.
- Added dynamic scheduling for load balancing.
- Reused per-thread workspaces to avoid hot-loop allocations.
- Incrementally updated cluster diameters instead of recomputing them.
- Preserved original seed-order tie-breaking across all thread counts.
- Made OpenMP a required CMake dependency; executable remains `qtclustering`.
